## Requirement extraction notes

The University of Michigan’s official BME graduate admissions page confirms official transcript upload, a statement of purpose, a personal statement, a CV or résumé, three letters, background-course expectations, and English proficiency treatment for both degree types. It also states that the GRE general test is neither required nor accepted for its M.S. and Ph.D. programs. The master’s-specific admissions page corroborates the materials and its 3.2 GPA guidance. These summarized requirements are stored in the profile while the detailed source URLs remain the authority for live application instructions.

## Admission snapshot field backfill

Purdue Biomedical Engineering thesis M.S. and Ph.D. Duolingo refresh completed: the current BME graduate-admission page explicitly adopts OGSPS English-proficiency requirements and states BME cannot waive them. Both records now match the already reconciled Professional M.S. wording: DET 115 overall and 115 in every integrated subscore, with scores less than two years old when the admission recommendation reaches OGSPS; the profiles retain the central-program caveat that a graduate program may require more.

Saint Louis University Biomedical Engineering Ph.D. Duolingo backfill completed: the current central international English-proficiency page sets a 110 Duolingo baseline for applicants and expressly allows graduate programs to impose higher minimums. The current BME doctorate requires English-proficiency evidence but does not supply a different DET value; its profile now uses the qualified central 110 wording rather than an invented program-specific cutoff.

University of Texas at Arlington Biomedical Engineering Ph.D. Duolingo backfill completed: the current graduate catalog and international graduate-admissions page independently state a Duolingo 100 minimum for graduate admission. Both sources explicitly preserve program-level authority to require or prefer higher scores; because the current BME doctorate does not publish a contrary threshold, the profile uses that qualified central baseline.

Oregon State University Bioengineering M.Eng. Duolingo backfill completed: the current Graduate Admissions international page sets a regular-admission Duolingo 110 baseline with subscores, permits graduate programs to set higher scores, and treats GTA-funded admission as case-by-case while a Duolingo speaking threshold remains undecided. The current Bioengineering M.Eng. source does not establish an exception, so the profile uses the bounded central policy.

University of Kansas Bioengineering M.S. and Ph.D. Duolingo backfill completed: the current KU Graduate Admissions FAQ explicitly says Duolingo is not accepted for English proficiency, while the contemporaneous English-requirements page lists TOEFL, IELTS-Academic, and PTE routes. Neither reviewed Bioengineering source establishes a contrary rule, so both degree paths now show the qualified central non-acceptance policy.

University of Akron Biomedical Engineering M.S.E. and M.B.E. Duolingo backfill completed: current Graduate Application Requirements establish DET 115, a two-year validity limit, and a DET restriction for prospective teaching-assistant qualification; the Graduate Bulletin independently preserves department authority to require higher standards. The M.B.E. uses the central baseline. The M.S.E. retains its separate higher TOEFL rule but does not invent a DET equivalent, so its profile displays the central baseline with an explicit caveat.

## Indiana cohort reconciliation — 2026-08-14

Purdue University Biomedical Engineering Ph.D. and thesis M.S. were refreshed against official Weldon School graduate admissions, degree requirements, funding, and departmental research-imagery sources. The profiles retain annual December 1 Fall and limited September 15 Spring guidance, qualified master’s/doctoral assistantship and fellowship availability, the direct Purdue Graduate application, and intentionally blank fee, GRE, English-test, and Duolingo snapshot fields where the BME-specific pages did not state a rule. Their hero source was replaced with a credited official Weldon School BME research image; both records now meet five source categories at three passes.

Purdue’s coursework-only Professional M.S. in Biomedical Engineering was published as a distinct degree. Official BME sources establish its AI and digital health, biomedical-device development, and clinical-engagement pathways; annual December 1 priority/rolling-April-15 Fall, September 15 Spring, and February 15 Summer guidance; no-GRE treatment; TOEFL 80/IELTS 6.5 international minimums; direct application; qualified master’s funding availability; and the credited official Weldon School image. A current fee is not stored because the reviewed rendered central page did not expose it.

Indiana University Indianapolis Biomedical Engineering M.S. and Ph.D. were separately published after reconciliation of degree pages, Luddy master’s/doctoral admissions, curriculum requirements, scholarships, and page-hosted INDI laboratory imagery. The Ph.D. has the same US$70 fee, blank source-safe GRE field, TOEFL 90/IELTS 7.0/Duolingo 130 treatment, and qualified fellowship support with tuition reimbursement and monthly stipend; no dated BME-specific deadline was inserted. Both records use an official admissions guide rather than an unverified external form and a credited, official INDI laboratory image. Each meets five source categories at three passes.

University of Notre Dame Bioengineering Ph.D. was published after reconciliation of the College of Engineering doctoral page, departmental application page, Graduate School funding page, and department-controlled research image. The profile uses annual December 31 Fall guidance, optional GRE treatment, direct Graduate School application, program-stated fellowship/RA support, intentionally blank unsupported language/fee fields, and the credited Bioengineering research image. Central doctoral requirements provide annual deadline guidance: December 1 fall priority, January 15 fall final, September 1 spring, March 1 summer by faculty invitation; application materials include transcripts, optional GRE, three letters, 400–600-word statement, CV, relevant English evidence, and $80 fee/eligible waiver. It prefers BME preparation but considers others with two physics and two mathematics courses and 3.0 GPA. GW says Ph.D. students are often offered full packages but departmental faculty/award decisions vary; M.S. students are considered for merit tuition discounts rather than guaranteed funding. |

## New Hampshire cohort reconciliation — 2026-08-14

Dartmouth Engineering’s formal Biomedical Engineering program area supports an Engineering Sciences Ph.D., and its on-campus M.Eng. has a separately presented Biomedical track. The doctoral profile records the current annual December 15 priority timing, US$75 fee, English and Duolingo guidance, direct application route, and official GRA/external-fellowship support treatment. The professional M.Eng. records its course-based, no-thesis Biomedical pathway; annual December 15 and February 15 rounds; optional GRE; US$75 fee; English/Duolingo guidance; partial-tuition-aid availability; and direct application route. Dartmouth’s generic research M.S. was reviewed but not published as a stand-alone biomedical degree because the official degree identity is Engineering Sciences rather than a distinct biomedical-named award.

University of New Hampshire began separately awarding Bioengineering M.Eng., M.S., and Ph.D. degrees in Fall 2025. The M.S. is thesis-based, the M.Eng. culminates in an engineering project, and the Ph.D. is research-intensive. All three use official annual deadlines, US$65 fee, current General GRE requirement, TOEFL/IELTS and Duolingo treatment, Graduate School application route, qualified assistantship availability, and credited Durham campus imagery. UNH’s distinct M.S. in Electrical and Computer Engineering with a Biomedical Engineering option was also published with its 31-credit thesis structure, ECE-specific conditional GRE waiver policy, annual guidance, and qualified ECE funding treatment. The initial UNH Bioengineering Ph.D. hero capture showed the normal gradient while its re-keyed asset resolved; delayed browser inspection confirmed the credited image element renders and the hero becomes visibly photographic after delivery. All six New Hampshire profiles meet five official source categories at three verification passes, credited imagery, and zero validation exceptions.

## Scope correction — Biological Engineering records removed, August 17, 2026

At the owner’s explicit direction, standalone **Biological Engineering** degree records were removed from the published directory because that title falls outside the tracker’s intended Biomedical Engineering, Bioengineering, Neuroengineering, and directly medical-device-related scope. The eight removed records were MIT; University of Hawaiʻi at Mānoa; University of Idaho (Ph.D., M.S., M.Engr.); and Utah State University (Ph.D., M.S., M.E.). A database check confirmed that none had a saved personal application, so the removal did not delete or orphan user progress. This scope rule applies prospectively to discovery and publication.

## Purdue Biomedical Engineering fee-waiver refresh, August 26, 2026
Purdue Engineering’s current Virtual Graduate Showcase provides a qualified 2026 application-fee-waiver route for the BME Ph.D. and thesis M.S. paths. The free online event is September 27–28, 2026; registrants must complete at least 10 Whova Resources > Survey questions (100 points) by October 12, 2026, 11:59 p.m. EDT. After the October 13 confirmation, an applicant must use the same email for the OGSPS application, select Purdue Engineering Virtual Graduate Showcase in the Recruitment Programs fee-waiver field, and submit the application. OGSPS verifies participation and emails the decision in 2–3 days; mismatched emails require manual confirmation through gradinfo@purdue.edu and can take longer during peak periods. ## Dartmouth Biomedical Engineering fee-waiver refresh, August 26, 2026
Dartmouth Engineering’s current admissions pages state that qualifying fee waivers are automatically applied once an application has been started for a Dartmouth Engineering program. Applicants with an ETS GRE fee-reduction voucher may request a waiver in the graduate application by uploading the ETS email PDF; Guarini decides within four business days. Voucher requests should be made at least one week before the program deadline, applicants must not submit before the waiver notice, and paid fees cannot be waived retroactively or refunded. The shared policy was applied to the in-scope Dartmouth Engineering Ph.D. in Engineering Sciences, Biomedical Engineering Area and M.Eng. in Engineering, Biomedical Track. Sources: https://engineering.dartmouth.edu/graduate-admissions/phd-admissions ; https://engineering.dartmouth.edu/graduate-admissions/ms-admissions ; https://graduate.dartmouth.edu/admissions/applying-dartmouth/fee-waiver-criteria.

## Joint UNC–NC State Biomedical Engineering fee-waiver boundary review, August 26, 2026
No joint UNC–NC State BME fee-waiver guidance was added in this pass. The current NC State Graduate Handbook allows a fee waiver only under exceptional circumstances and points to a Graduate School knowledge-base page for the process. That linked page does not expose eligibility categories, documentation, submission mechanics, timing, or a usable public request route. The current joint BME admissions FAQ says an application is forwarded to the department after the Graduate School receives payment and gives coordinator contacts, but it does not publish a BME waiver exception. The central exceptional-circumstances wording is therefore not shown as an actionable BME fee-waiver panel for the doctoral or MedTech master’s path. Sources: https://catalog.ncsu.edu/graduate/graduate-handbook/applications/ ; https://ncsu.service-now.com/gradschool?id=kb_article&sys_id=084f93b847ff25544c529069736d43b0 ; https://bme.unc.edu/graduate/graduate-admissions-faq/.

## UMass Amherst Biomedical Engineering fee-waiver evidence, August 26, 2026
UMass Amherst’s current BME doctoral and master’s pages publish a comprehensive Graduate School fee-waiver framework, including McNair documentation, a GRE-fee-reduction submission, Five College master’s-only automatic treatment, internal UMass master’s-to-same-program-doctoral automatic treatment, and qualified domestic recruitment-program routes. However, the active directory contains no published UMass Amherst BME Ph.D. or M.S. profile to which this evidence can be applied. No new program was created during this narrow fee-waiver pass; this verified evidence is logged for a future, separately scoped program-publication reconciliation. Sources: https://www.umass.edu/engineering/academics/phd-biomedical-engineering/bme-phd-application-notes ; https://www.umass.edu/engineering/academics/master-science-biomedical-engineering/application-notes-ms-bme ; https://www.umass.edu/graduate/apply/how-apply ; https://www.umass.edu/graduate/office-inclusion-engagement/spaulding-smith-fellowship/information-facultystaff/recruitment.
